## Speed pack

Interpretation: higher speed is better; delta uses `rust_bytes_per_sec / ffi_bytes_per_sec` when throughput exists, otherwise fallback is `ffi_ms_per_iter / rust_ms_per_iter`.

### Rust speed

## Speed pack

Interpretation: higher speed is better; delta uses `rust_bytes_per_sec / ffi_bytes_per_sec` when throughput exists, otherwise fallback is `ffi_ms_per_iter / rust_ms_per_iter`.

### Rust speed
